## Reading the lists

Ranked within each category by **DR4**, then **DR3**, highest first. Ties keep the source order. DR4 and DR3 are Ahrefs Domain Rating models; they are not consecutive measurement periods. Missing ratings appear as —.

Each platform name links to its website.

Favicons are served by [Awesome Indie](https://awesomeindie.com). When the source has no favicon, the platform's initials are shown, matching the original directory.

Add a row to the table below and open a pull request. Link the platform name to its website, choose a category from the index above, and include a short description. For submission cost, use **Free**, **Free + paid**, or **Paid**, or leave it blank if unknown. Favicons and DR scores are not required.

I'll review suggestions and add accepted platforms to the relevant category in this repository and to [Awesome Indie](https://awesomeindie.com/where-to-launch-your-startup).
